## shot_01 — Omni 1.1 Flash (độ khó 2/10)

- First frame (`KF_CTRV1_S01_START`): `assets/frames/KF_CTRV1_S01_START.jpg`

```text
1.2m ground-level 35mm Anamorphic wide shot, steady locked-off framing. Rex is a massive 9-ton adult Tyrannosaurus rex with warm earthy bronze-brown pebbly hide, golden-ochre underbelly, thick smooth muscular back without dorsal spikes, broad rectangular bone-crushing skull, and deep-set amber reptile eyes (current state/wardrobe: dry bronze-brown hide dusted with forest soil, dry muscular legs, alert full-stamina posture). Choreography timeline: [0.0s-2.5s Entry State & Telegraph] Camera: 1.2m ground-level 35mm Anamorphic wide shot, steady locked-off framing. Action: Rex walks from left to center across the dry coastal clearing, dry soil crunching underfoot with the muddy marsh and ocean surf visible in the far right background. VFX/Env: Contiguous terrain physics in ENV_ZONE1_DRY_CLEARING, realistic surface interaction. SFX: 50 Hz sub-bass footstep thuds on dry earth, dry pebbles rattling, distant ocean surf. [2.5s-5.5s Primary Biomechanical Action] Camera: 1.2m ground-level 35mm Anamorphic wide shot, steady locked-off framing. Action: Rex strides across the dry earth clearing, then halts mid-step and snaps its massive head toward the right as heavy rival footsteps vibrate the dry pebbles. VFX/Env: Heavy 9-ton mass coupling, realistic dirt/mud/seawater displacement. SFX: 50 Hz sub-bass footstep thuds on dry earth, dry pebbles rattling, distant ocean surf. [5.5s-8.0s Consequence & Exit State Lock] Camera: 1.2m ground-level 35mm Anamorphic wide shot, steady locked-off framing. Action: Rex stands braced on the left side of the dry clearing facing sharply right, nostrils flared and amber eyes locked on the tree line. VFX/Env: Settling debris, mud splatters, or ocean wave foam matching exit state. SFX: 50 Hz sub-bass footstep thuds on dry earth, dry pebbles rattling, distant ocean surf. A dry prehistoric coastal redwood clearing with hard-packed earth, scattered river cobbles, ancient fern undergrowth on the left, and a contiguous downhill slope on the right leading directly into a dark muddy marsh and the breaking ocean surf beyond. Live-action IMAX 65mm anamorphic creature cinematography, physical contact mass coupling, golden-amber twilight sunbeams piercing cold 6500K blue-teal coastal mist, wet subsurface-scattered reptile hide, 180-degree shutter natural motion blur, fine 35mm Kodak Vision3 film grain. Ambient sound only: 50 Hz sub-bass footstep thuds on dry earth, dry pebbles rattling, distant ocean surf. No subtitles, no on-screen text.
```
